Result: 实验表明：1) 过多的结构上下文会引入噪声；2) 基于规则的指令提示能提高评估可靠性；3) 所得可信度分数能够区分安全与易受攻击的实现，支持使用LLM进行可扩展、上下文感知的可信度评估。

Conclusion: LLM可用于自动化Web应用可信度评估，验证安全编码实践遵循情况。提示工程策略（特别是基于规则的指令提示）对评估可靠性至关重要，层次质量模型能够提供整体可信度分数，为可扩展的上下文感知评估提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The widespread adoption of web applications has made their security a critical concern and has increased the need for systematic ways to assess whether they can be considered trustworthy. However, "trust" assessment remains an open problem as existing techniques primarily focus on detecting known vulnerabilities or depend on manual evaluation, which limits their scalability; therefore, evaluating adherence to secure coding practices offers a complementary, pragmatic perspective by focusing on observable development behaviors. In practice, the identification and verification of secure coding practices are predominantly performed manually, relying on expert knowledge and code reviews, which is time-consuming, subjective, and difficult to scale. This study presents an empirical methodology to automate the trustworthiness assessment of web applications by leveraging Large Language Models (LLMs) to verify adherence to secure coding practices. We conduct a comparative analysis of prompt engineering techniques across five state-of-the-art LLMs, ranging from baseline zero-shot classification to prompts enriched with semantic definitions, structural context derived from call graphs, and explicit instructional guidance. Furthermore, we propose an extension of a hierarchical Quality Model (QM) based on the Logic Score of Preference (LSP), in which LLM outputs are used to populate the model's quality attributes and compute a holistic trustworthiness score. Experimental results indicate that excessive structural context can introduce noise, whereas rule-based instructional prompting improves assessment reliability. The resulting trustworthiness score allows discriminating between secure and vulnerable implementations, supporting the feasibility of using LLMs for scalable and context-aware trust assessment.

</details>


### [113] [How Vulnerable Are Edge LLMs?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23822)
*Ao Ding,Hongzong Li,Zi Liang,Zhanpeng Shi,Shuxin Zhuang,Shiqin Tang,Rong Feng,Ping Lu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 量化部署在边缘设备上的大语言模型仍面临查询式知识提取风险，CLIQ框架能有效恢复模型语义知识


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究量化边缘部署LLMs的安全隐患，特别是查询式知识提取风险，量化虽然引入噪声但未消除语义知识

Method: 提出CLIQ（聚类指令查询）框架，通过结构化查询构建提高语义覆盖并减少冗余，在量化Qwen模型（INT8/INT4）上进行实验

Result: CLIQ在BERTScore、BLEU和ROUGE指标上持续优于原始查询，在有限查询预算下实现更高效的知识提取

Conclusion: 量化本身不能有效防御查询式知识提取攻击，揭示了边缘部署LLMs中一个先前未被充分探索的安全风险

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed on edge devices under strict computation and quantization constraints, yet their security implications remain unclear. We study query-based knowledge extraction from quantized edge-deployed LLMs under realistic query budgets and show that, although quantization introduces noise, it does not remove the underlying semantic knowledge, allowing substantial behavioral recovery through carefully designed queries. To systematically analyze this risk, we propose \textbf{CLIQ} (\textbf{Cl}ustered \textbf{I}nstruction \textbf{Q}uerying), a structured query construction framework that improves semantic coverage while reducing redundancy. Experiments on quantized Qwen models (INT8/INT4) demonstrate that CLIQ consistently outperforms original queries across BERTScore, BLEU, and ROUGE, enabling more efficient extraction under limited budgets. These results indicate that quantization alone does not provide effective protection against query-based extraction, highlighting a previously underexplored security risk in edge-deployed LLMs.

</details>


### [114] [An Empirical Analysis of Google Play Data Safety Disclosures: A Consistency Study of Privacy Indicators in Mobile Gaming Apps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23935)
*Bakheet Aljedaani*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该研究通过静态分析对比41款移动游戏应用的APK隐私指标与Google Play数据安全标签的开发者报告，发现两者存在显著不一致，特别是位置相关披露的不一致率达56.1%，表明当前市场透明度机制存在可靠性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Google Play引入的数据安全部分依赖开发者自我报告隐私实践，其准确性存在担忧。研究旨在调查开发者报告的数据安全披露与实际从APK提取的可观察隐私指标之间的一致性，评估当前市场透明度机制的可靠性。

Method: 采用静态分析方法从41款移动游戏应用的APK文件中提取关键隐私指标（设备ID、数据共享、个人信息访问、位置访问），通过结构化一致性评估框架系统比较这些指标与Google Play数据安全标签中的相应披露，并使用卡方统计检验分析应用类别间的差异。

Result: 不同隐私类别的一致性水平存在差异：设备ID披露一致性相对较高（87.8%），而其他指标存在显著不匹配。位置相关披露不一致率最高（56.1%），其次是个人信息和数据共享。儿童导向应用与普通受众应用的不匹配模式相似，卡方检验表明这些差异无统计学意义，说明披露不一致与应用类别无关，而是反映了更广泛的生态系统层面挑战。

Conclusion: 研究发现当前市场透明度机制在可靠性方面存在局限性，强调了需要改进验证方法以确保移动应用生态系统中隐私报告的准确性。披露不一致问题普遍存在且与特定应用类别无关，表明需要更有效的监督和验证机制来增强用户隐私保护。

Abstract: The Google Play marketplace has introduced the Data Safety section to improve transparency regarding how mobile applications (apps) collect, share, and protect user data. This mechanism requires developers to disclose privacy and security-related practices. However, the reliability of these disclosures remains dependent on developer self-reporting, raising concerns about their accuracy. This study investigates the consistency between developer-reported Data Safety disclosures and observable privacy indicators extracted from Android Application Packages (APKs). An empirical analysis was conducted on a dataset of 41 mobile gaming apps. A static analysis approach was used to extract key privacy indicators from APK files, including device IDs, data sharing, personal information access, and location access. These indicators were systematically compared with the corresponding disclosures reported in the Google Play Data Safety labels using a structured consistency evaluation framework. The results revealed varying levels of agreement across privacy categories. Device ID disclosures demonstrated relatively high consistency (87.8%), whereas other indicators exhibited substantial mismatches. Location-related disclosures showed the highest inconsistency rate (56.1%), followed by personal information and data sharing. Comparative analysis between children-oriented and general-audience apps revealed similar mismatch patterns. Also, Chi-square statistical tests indicate that these differences are not statistically significant, suggesting that disclosure inconsistencies are not associated with app category but instead reflect broader ecosystem-level challenges. These findings highlight limitations in the reliability of current marketplace transparency mechanisms and emphasize the need for improved validation and verification approaches to ensure accurate privacy reporting in mobile app ecosystems.

</details>


### [115] [Walma: Learning to See Memory Corruption in WebAssembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24167)
*Oussama Draissi,Mark Günzel,Ahmad-Reza Sadeghi,Lucas Davi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Walma框架利用机器学习对WebAssembly内存快照进行分类，检测内存损坏和外部篡改，在结构化内存布局应用中有效，粗粒度边界检查仅带来1.07倍开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WebAssembly的单片线性内存模型容易受到内存损坏攻击，可能导致浏览器中的跨站脚本攻击，或在恶意主机篡改模块状态时不被检测。现有防御依赖于侵入式二进制插桩或自定义运行时，且未解决对抗性主机模型下的运行时完整性验证问题。

Method: 提出Walma框架，用于WebAssembly线性内存认证，利用机器学习对内存快照进行分类来检测内存损坏和外部篡改。在三个验证后端（cpu-wasm、cpu-tch、gpu）和三种插桩策略下，对六个受CVE影响的实际应用进行评估。

Result: 基于CNN的分类能有效检测具有结构化内存布局应用中的内存损坏，粗粒度边界检查开销低至1.07倍，细粒度监控引入较高（1.5x-1.8x）但可预测的成本。评估量化了不同部署配置下的准确性和开销权衡。

Conclusion: 基于机器学习的内存认证在WebAssembly中具有实际可行性，Walma框架在检测内存损坏和外部篡改方面表现出色，同时提供了可接受的开销成本。

Abstract: WebAssembly's (Wasm) monolithic linear memory model facilitates memory corruption attacks that can escalate to cross-site scripting in browsers or go undetected when a malicious host tampers with a module's state. Existing defenses rely on invasive binary instrumentation or custom runtimes, and do not address runtime integrity verification under an adversarial host model. We present Walma, a framework for WebAssembly Linear Memory Attestation that leverages machine learning to detect memory corruption and external tampering by classifying memory snapshots. We evaluate Walma on six real-world CVE-affected applications across three verification backends (cpu-wasm, cpu-tch, gpu) and three instrumentation policies. Our results demonstrate that CNN-based classification can effectively detect memory corruption in applications with structured memory layouts, with coarse-grained boundary checks incurring as low as 1.07x overhead, while fine-grained monitoring introduces higher (1.5x--1.8x) but predictable costs. Our evaluation quantifies the accuracy and overhead trade-offs across deployment configurations, demonstrating the practical feasibility of ML-based memory attestation for WebAssembly.

</details>


### [116] [Towards Remote Attestation of Microarchitectural Attacks: The Case of Rowhammer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24172)
*Martin Herrmann,Oussama Draissi,Christian Niesler,Lucas Davi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: HammerWatch：一种基于远程证明的Rowhammer检测协议，通过微架构感知的远程证明来检测硬件引发的干扰行为，而非依赖预防机制


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微架构漏洞日益削弱硬件作为可信根的假设，预防机制往往滞后于攻击技术的发展，导致已部署系统无法保证持续可信。需要从预防转向检测，通过微架构感知的远程证明来评估系统的硬件安全性。

Method: 提出HammerWatch协议，利用商用平台上的内存级证据：ECC DRAM的机器检查异常（MCE）和每行激活计数（PRAC）的基于计数器的指标，使用TPM锚定的哈希链保护这些测量值免受内核级攻击者的篡改。

Result: 在商用硬件上实现HammerWatch，并在20000个模拟的良性和恶意访问模式上进行评估。结果表明，在保守启发式下，验证者能够可靠地区分Rowhammer类行为和良性操作。

Conclusion: 检测导向的远程证明是可行的，可以补充不完整的预防机制。HammerWatch作为微架构感知远程证明的第一个实例，展示了通过远程证明检测硬件漏洞的潜力。

Abstract: Microarchitectural vulnerabilities increasingly undermine the assumption that hardware can be treated as a reliable root of trust. Prevention mechanisms often lag behind evolving attack techniques, leaving deployed systems unable to assume continued trustworthiness. We propose a shift from prevention to detection through microarchitectural-aware remote attestation. As a first instantiation of this idea, we present HammerWatch, a Rowhammer-aware remote attestation protocol that enables an external verifier to assess whether a system exhibits hardware-induced disturbance behavior. HammerWatch leverages memory-level evidence available on commodity platforms, specifically Machine-Check Exceptions (MCEs) from ECC DRAM and counter-based indicators from Per-Row Activation Counting (PRAC), and protects these measurements against kernel-level adversaries using TPM-anchored hash chains. We implement HammerWatch on commodity hardware and evaluate it on 20000 simulated benign and malicious access patterns. Our results show that the verifier reliably distinguishes Rowhammer-like behavior from benign operation under conservative heuristics, demonstrating that detection-oriented attestation is feasible and can complement incomplete prevention mechanisms

</details>


### [117] [Analysing the Safety Pitfalls of Steering Vectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24543)
*Yuxiao Li,Alina Fastowski,Efstratios Zaradoukas,Bardh Prenkaj,Gjergji Kasneci*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 对CAA激活导向向量进行系统性安全审计，发现导向向量能显著影响越狱攻击成功率（增加达57%或减少达50%），揭示了LLM可控性与安全性之间的权衡


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活导向已成为无需权重更新即可塑造LLM行为的强大工具，但其固有的脆弱性和不可靠性已被充分记录，而安全性影响尚未得到充分探索。本研究旨在系统审计广泛使用的对比激活加法（CAA）导向向量的安全性影响

Method: 使用JailbreakBench作为基准，在统一评估协议下对CAA导向向量进行系统性安全审计。通过分析导向向量对越狱攻击成功率的影响，并探究导向向量与拒绝行为潜在方向的重叠关系

Result: 导向向量能一致性地影响越狱攻击成功率，在简单模板攻击下放大效应更强。在不同LLM家族和规模中，沿特定方向导向模型可显著增加（最高57%）或降低（最高50%）攻击成功率，具体取决于目标行为。这种现象归因于导向向量与拒绝行为潜在方向的重叠

Conclusion: 研究揭示了LLM中先前未观察到的安全性差距的起源，突显了可控性与安全性之间的权衡。导向向量与拒绝行为潜在方向的重叠为这一发现提供了可追溯的解释

Abstract: Activation steering has emerged as a powerful tool to shape LLM behavior without the need for weight updates. While its inherent brittleness and unreliability are well-documented, its safety implications remain underexplored. In this work, we present a systematic safety audit of steering vectors obtained with Contrastive Activation Addition (CAA), a widely used steering approach, under a unified evaluation protocol. Using JailbreakBench as benchmark, we show that steering vectors consistently influence the success rate of jailbreak attacks, with stronger amplification under simple template-based attacks. Across LLM families and sizes, steering the model in specific directions can drastically increase (up to 57%) or decrease (up to 50%) its attack success rate (ASR), depending on the targeted behavior. We attribute this phenomenon to the overlap between the steering vectors and the latent directions of refusal behavior. Thus, we offer a traceable explanation for this discovery. Together, our findings reveal the previously unobserved origin of this safety gap in LLMs, highlighting a trade-off between controllability and safety.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [118] [PDGMM-VAE: A Variational Autoencoder with Adaptive Per-Dimension Gaussian Mixture Model Priors for Nonlinear ICA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23547)
*Yuan-Hao Wei,Yan-Jie Sun*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: PDGMM-VAE：一种源导向的变分自编码器，为每个潜在维度（解释为独立源信号）分配独立的高斯混合模型先验，通过自适应学习先验参数实现盲源分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统VAE使用共享简单先验无法有效捕捉不同源信号的非高斯统计特性，限制了在盲源分离中的应用。需要为每个潜在源信号设计独立的先验约束以促进源分离。

Method: 提出PDGMM-VAE框架：1) 每个潜在维度（对应一个源信号）分配独立的高斯混合模型先验；2) 先验参数与编码器、解码器参数共同自适应学习；3) 编码器作为解混映射从观测到潜在源，解码器从推断分量重建观测混合。

Result: 在线性和非线性混合问题上，PDGMM-VAE能够有效恢复潜在源信号并实现满意的分离性能，验证了为不同潜在源配备不同GMM先验的ICA方法的有效性。

Conclusion: PDGMM-VAE通过为每个潜在维度分配自适应学习的GMM先验，成功将源导向的ICA思想形式化为完整的VAE框架，实现了端到端训练和先验学习，为盲源分离提供了新的概率编码器-解码器架构。

Abstract: Independent component analysis is a core framework within blind source separation for recovering latent source signals from observed mixtures under statistical independence assumptions. In this work, we propose PDGMM-VAE, a source-oriented variational autoencoder in which each latent dimension, interpreted explicitly as an individual source signal, is assigned its own Gaussian mixture model prior. Unlike conventional VAE formulations with a shared simple prior, the proposed framework imposes per-dimension heterogeneous prior constraints, enabling the model to capture diverse non-Gaussian source statistics and thereby promote source separation under a probabilistic encoder-decoder architecture. Importantly, the parameters of these per-dimension GMM priors are not fixed in advance, but are adaptively learned and automatically refined toward convergence together with the encoder and decoder parameters under the overall training objective. Within this formulation, the encoder serves as a demixing mapping from observations to latent sources, while the decoder reconstructs the observed mixtures from the inferred components. The proposed model provides a systematic study of an idea that had previously only been noted in our preliminary form, namely, equipping different latent sources with different GMM priors for ICA, and formulates it as a full VAE framework with end-to-end training and per-dimension prior learning. Experimental results on both linear and nonlinear mixing problems demonstrate that PDGMM-VAE can recover latent source signals and achieve satisfactory separation performance.

</details>


### [119] [The Mass Agreement Score: A Point-centric Measure of Cluster Size Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23581)
*Randolph Wiredu-Aidoo*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出一种新的聚类均匀性度量方法——质量一致性分数(MAS)，用于评估聚类划分中簇大小的均匀性，具有稳定性、片段鲁棒性和点中心特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在聚类分析中，某个簇的规模过大往往是不理想的，需要一种能够衡量簇大小均匀性的度量标准来筛选这样的划分。现有基于标签的度量方法存在不稳定性问题，因为聚类算法可能产生不同数量的标签，即使底层点分布变化很小。

Method: 提出质量一致性分数(MAS)，这是一种点中心度量方法，范围在[0,1]之间。它从每个簇中点的视角评估预期簇大小的一致性。通过设计实现片段鲁棒性，对具有相似主体结构的划分分配相似分数，同时对真正的簇质量重新分布保持敏感性。

Result: MAS度量具有以下特性：1) 稳定性：点分配略有不同的划分获得相似均匀性分数；2) 片段鲁棒性：对标签数量扰动具有鲁棒性；3) 点中心性：从点的视角评估簇大小一致性；4) 有界性：分数范围在[0,1]之间。

Conclusion: MAS提供了一种稳定、鲁棒的聚类均匀性度量方法，解决了传统基于标签的度量方法在标签数量变化时的不稳定性问题，能够有效识别和过滤具有过度主导簇的聚类划分。

Abstract: In clustering, strong dominance in the size of a particular cluster is often undesirable, motivating a measure of cluster size uniformity that can be used to filter such partitions. A basic requirement of such a measure is stability: partitions that differ only slightly in their point assignments should receive similar uniformity scores. A difficulty arises because cluster labels are not fixed objects; algorithms may produce different numbers of labels even when the underlying point distribution changes very little. Measures defined directly over labels can therefore become unstable under label-count perturbations. I introduce the Mass Agreement Score (MAS), a point-centric metric bounded in [0, 1] that evaluates the consistency of expected cluster size as measured from the perspective of points in each cluster. Its construction yields fragment robustness by design, assigning similar scores to partitions with similar bulk structure while remaining sensitive to genuine redistribution of cluster mass.

</details>


### [120] [Wasserstein Parallel Transport for Predicting the Dynamics of Statistical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23736)
*Tristan Luca Saidi,Gonzalo Mena,Larry Wasserman,Florian Gunsilius*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出"Wasserstein Parallel Trends"方法，通过最优传输测地线上的平行输运来比较分布动态，用于因果推断、域适应和反事实预测


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多科学系统（如细胞群体、经济队列）随时间演化的概率分布缺乏向量空间结构，传统方法依赖的向量减法不适用，需要新的分布层面动态比较方法

Method: 基于最优传输测地线的切向量平行输运原理，提出Wasserstein平行趋势概念，用测地线平行输运替代经典向量减法，建立分布动态反事实比较框架

Result: 提出了Wasserstein流形上的新型扇形方案，可高效近似测地线平行输运并提供理论保证；方法可恢复经典平行趋势假设作为特例，推导出高斯测度的闭式平行输运；在合成数据和单细胞RNA测序数据集上验证了基因表达动态推断

Conclusion: Wasserstein平行趋势为分布动态比较提供了统一框架，适用于因果推断、域适应和实验批次效应校正，扩展了经典方法到分布空间

Abstract: Many scientific systems, such as cellular populations or economic cohorts, are naturally described by probability distributions that evolve over time. Predicting how such a system would have evolved under different forces or initial conditions is fundamental to causal inference, domain adaptation, and counterfactual prediction. However, the space of distributions often lacks the vector space structure on which classical methods rely. To address this, we introduce a general notion of parallel dynamics at a distributional level. We base this principle on parallel transport of tangent dynamics along optimal transport geodesics and call it ``Wasserstein Parallel Trends''. By replacing the vector subtraction of classic methods with geodesic parallel transport, we can provide counterfactual comparisons of distributional dynamics in applications such as causal inference, domain adaptation, and batch-effect correction in experimental settings. The main mathematical contribution is a novel notion of fanning scheme on the Wasserstein manifold that allows us to efficiently approximate parallel transport along geodesics while also providing the first theoretical guarantees for parallel transport in the Wasserstein space. We also show that Wasserstein Parallel Trends recovers the classic parallel trends assumption for averages as a special case and derive closed-form parallel transport for Gaussian measures. We deploy the method on synthetic data and two single-cell RNA sequencing datasets to impute gene-expression dynamics across biological systems.

</details>


### [121] [Federated fairness-aware classification under differential privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24392)
*Gengyu Xue,Yi Yu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文研究了联邦学习场景下差分隐私与公平性的联合影响，提出了FDP-Fair和CDP-Fair两种算法，在保证隐私和公平性的同时控制超额风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐私保护和算法公平性已成为机器学习中的两个核心问题，但它们的联合影响在联邦学习场景下尚未得到充分研究。论文旨在系统性地探索在数据分布在多个服务器的联邦设置中，差分隐私与公平性约束对分类任务的共同影响。

Method: 针对联邦差分隐私下的群体差异约束分类问题，提出了FDP-Fair两阶段算法。在单服务器特殊情况下，提出了计算轻量的CDP-Fair算法作为替代方案。两种算法均在温和的结构假设下建立了隐私、公平性和超额风险控制的理论保证。

Result: 理论分析将隐私公平感知的超额风险分解为四个来源：分类固有成本、隐私分类成本、非隐私公平成本和隐私公平成本。在合成和真实数据集上的大量实验验证了所设计算法的实用性。

Conclusion: 该研究为联邦学习场景下同时保证隐私和公平性的分类问题提供了系统性的理论框架和实用算法，揭示了隐私与公平性约束对模型性能的联合影响机制。

Abstract: Privacy and algorithmic fairness have become two central issues in modern machine learning. Although each has separately emerged as a rapidly growing research area, their joint effect remains comparatively under-explored. In this paper, we systematically study the joint impact of differential privacy and fairness on classification in a federated setting, where data are distributed across multiple servers. Targeting demographic disparity constrained classification under federated differential privacy, we propose a two-step algorithm, namely FDP-Fair. In the special case where there is only one server, we further propose a simple yet powerful algorithm, namely CDP-Fair, serving as a computationally-lightweight alternative. Under mild structural assumptions, theoretical guarantees on privacy, fairness and excess risk control are established. In particular, we disentangle the source of the private fairness-aware excess risk into a) intrinsic cost of classification, b) cost of private classification, c) non-private cost of fairness and d) private cost of fairness. Our theoretical findings are complemented by extensive numerical experiments on both synthetic and real datasets, highlighting the practicality of our designed algorithms.

</details>


### [122] [Continuous-Time Learning of Probability Distributions: A Case Study in a Digital Trial of Young Children with Type 1 Diabetes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24427)
*Antonio Álvarez-López,Marcos Matabuena*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出基于神经ODE的混合高斯模型，用于建模连续血糖监测数据的分布随时间演化，能检测传统方法难以捕捉的细微分布变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解生物标志物分布随时间演化是数字健康和慢性病监测的核心挑战。在糖尿病中，葡萄糖测量分布的变化可以揭示疾病进展和治疗反应的模式，这些模式是传统汇总指标所遗漏的。基于一项为期26周的临床试验（比较闭环胰岛素输送系统t:slim X2与标准疗法在儿童1型糖尿病中的效果），需要建模连续血糖监测数据的分布演化。

Method: 提出概率框架，使用神经ODE建模时间索引分布的连续时间演化。将葡萄糖分布表示为高斯混合模型，其中时变混合权重由神经ODE控制。使用基于最大平均差异的分布匹配准则估计模型参数。

Result: 该框架具有可解释性、计算效率高，并能敏感地检测细微的时间分布变化。应用于连续血糖监测试验数据时，该方法检测到治疗相关的葡萄糖动态改善，这些改善难以用传统分析方法捕捉。

Conclusion: 提出的基于神经ODE的混合高斯模型框架能有效建模连续血糖监测数据的分布演化，为糖尿病监测提供了更敏感的分析工具，能检测传统方法难以发现的细微治疗效应。

Abstract: Understanding how biomarker distributions evolve over time is a central challenge in digital health and chronic disease monitoring. In diabetes, changes in the distribution of glucose measurements can reveal patterns of disease progression and treatment response that conventional summary measures miss. Motivated by a 26-week clinical trial comparing the closed-loop insulin delivery system t:slim X2 with standard therapy in children with type 1 diabetes, we propose a probabilistic framework to model the continuous-time evolution of time-indexed distributions using continuous glucose monitoring data (CGM) collected every five minutes. We represent the glucose distribution as a Gaussian mixture, with time-varying mixture weights governed by a neural ODE. We estimate the model parameter using a distribution-matching criterion based on the maximum mean discrepancy. The resulting framework is interpretable, computationally efficient, and sensitive to subtle temporal distributional changes. Applied to CGM trial data, the method detects treatment-related improvements in glucose dynamics that are difficult to capture with traditional analytical approaches.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [123] [The Bernstein-von Mises theorem and efficiency for semiparametric inference in multivariate Hawkes processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23655)
*Mael Duverger,Judith Rousseau*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 该论文研究了多元Hawkes过程的半参数推断，建立了卷积定理和贝叶斯Bernstein-von Mises定理，证明了半参数贝叶斯方法具有渐近最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多元Hawkes过程广泛用于描述自激发和互激发现象，但现有的半参数推断理论不够完善。需要建立更一般的理论框架来研究这类过程的统计推断性质，特别是贝叶斯方法的渐近行为。

Method: 1. 建立卷积定理，给出光滑泛函正则估计量的最优极限分布；2. 在贝叶斯框架下，证明非参数随机级数先验的半参数Bernstein-von Mises定理；3. 将该结果应用于直方图和基于小波的先验；4. 结合卷积定理和BvM定理分析半参数贝叶斯程序的渐近性质。

Result: 1. 证明了卷积定理，确定了光滑泛函估计的最优极限分布；2. 证明了随机级数先验的Bernstein-von Mises定理；3. 证明了半参数贝叶斯程序具有渐近最优行为；4. 补充了Donnet等人(2020)的结果，证明了L2后验收缩。

Conclusion: 该研究为多元Hawkes过程的半参数推断提供了完整的理论框架，证明了贝叶斯方法在渐近意义下的最优性，为实际应用提供了理论保证，并扩展了随机级数先验的理论基础。

Abstract: In this paper, we study semiparametric inference for linear multivariate Hawkes processes, a class of point processes widely used to describe self and mutually exciting phenomena. We establish a convolution theorem giving the best limiting distribution for a regular estimator of smooth functional. Then, in the Bayesian setting, we prove a semiparametric Bernstein-von Mises (BvM) theorem for nonparametric random series priors. We apply this result to histogram and wavelet based priors. Taken together, the convolution and BvM theorems show that, from a frequentist point of view, semiparametric Bayesian procedures have asymptotically the optimal behavior. Deriving the BvM property for random series priors led us to prove L2 posterior contraction, complementing for these priors the results of Donnet, Rivoirard and Rousseau (2020).

</details>


### [124] [Microergodicity implies orthogonality of Matérn fields on bounded domains in $\mathbb{R}^4$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23959)
*Natesh S. Pillai*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 解决了平稳Matérn高斯随机场在d=4维情况下的固定域可识别性问题，证明了当微遍历参数相等但尺度参数不同时，对应的Gaussian测度是相互奇异的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Matérn随机场是空间统计学中最广泛使用的模型之一。平稳Matérn高斯随机场的协方差参数固定域可识别性存在维度依赖的相变：已知当d≤3时，具有相同微遍历参数的Matérn模型在有界域上诱导等价的Gaussian测度；当d>4时，参数不同时对应的测度相互奇异。临界情况d=4一直未解决，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用谱分析方法，可视为Anderes高阶增量方法的谱类比。通过局部化傅里叶系数检测高频谱不匹配，使用归一化Whittle得分来识别参数。具体来说，在d=4情况下，对于具有相同微遍历参数但尺度参数不同的两个平稳Matérn模型，证明了在任何有界观测域上，对应的Gaussian测度在可数稠密集和连续函数路径空间上都是相互奇异的。

Result: 成功解决了d=4的临界情况：当σ₁²α₁²ν = σ₂²α₂²ν且α₁ ≠ α₂时，对应的Gaussian测度在任何有界观测域上都是相互奇异的。这一结果完善了Matérn随机场固定域可识别性的维度相变理论。

Conclusion: 本文填补了Matérn随机场固定域可识别性维度相变理论中的关键空白，证明了d=4时与d>4情况相同的奇异性质。所提出的局部化谱探测框架为研究其他空间模型中协方差差异的检测提供了新方法，对可识别性和估计问题具有更广泛的适用性。

Abstract: Matérn random fields are one of the most widely used classes of models in spatial statistics. The fixed-domain identifiability of covariance parameters for stationary Matérn Gaussian random fields exhibits a dimension-dependent phase transition. For known smoothness $ν$, Zhang \cite{Zhang2004} showed that when $d\le3$, two Matérn models with the same microergodic parameter $m=σ^2α^{2ν}$ induce equivalent Gaussian measures on bounded domains, while Anderes \cite{Anderes2010} proved that when $d>4$, the corresponding measures are mutually singular whenever the parameters differ. The critical case $d=4$ for stationary Matérn models has remained open.
  We resolve this case. Let $d=4$ and consider two stationary Matérn models on $\mathbb R^4$ with parameters $(σ_1,α_1)$ and $(σ_2,α_2)$ satisfying \[ σ_1^2α_1^{2ν}=σ_2^2α_2^{2ν}, \qquad α_1\neq α_2. \] We prove that the corresponding Gaussian measures on any bounded observation domain are mutually singular on every countable dense observation set, and on the associated path space of continuous functions.
  Our approach can be viewed as a spectral analogue of the higher-order increment method of Anderes \cite{Anderes2010}. Whereas Anderes isolates the second irregular covariance coefficient through renormalized quadratic variations in physical space, we detect the first nonvanishing high-frequency spectral mismatch via localized Fourier coefficients and use a normalized Whittle score to identify parameters. More broadly, the localized spectral probing framework used here for detecting subtle covariance differences in Gaussian random fields may be useful for studying identifiability and estimation in other spatial models.

</details>


### [125] [Penalized estimation of GEV parameters for extreme quantile regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24153)
*Lucien M. Vidagbandji,Alexandre Berred,Cyrille Bertelle,Laurent Amanton*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 提出一种结合广义极值分布和广义随机森林的极端分位数回归方法，解决高概率水平下数据稀缺和复杂预测结构问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分位数回归在高概率水平面临尾部数据稀缺导致的推断困难，同时当预测变量多且分位数函数结构复杂时也存在挑战

Method: 使用广义极值分布建模块最大值，其参数依赖于协变量；采用广义随机森林估计参数，通过加权惩罚似然最大化来克服小样本下MLE的局限性

Result: 在模拟研究和美国工资数据应用中验证了该方法相对于其他方法的有效性

Conclusion: 提出的方法成功解决了极端分位数回归中的尾部推断和复杂结构问题，结合了极值理论和机器学习方法的优势

Abstract: Quantile regression (QR) relies on the estimation of conditional quantiles and explores the relationships between independent and dependent variables. At high probability levels, classical QR methods face extrapolation difficulties due to the scarcity of data in the tail of the distribution. Another challenge arises when the number of predictors is large and the quantile function exhibits a complex structure. In this work, we propose an estimation method designed to overcome these challenges. To enhance extrapolation in the tail of the conditional response distribution, we model block maxima using the generalized extreme value (GEV) distribution, where the parameters depend on covariates. To address the second challenge, we adopt an approach based on generalized random forests (grf) to estimate these parameters. Specifically, we maximize a penalized likelihood, weighted by the weights obtained through the grf method. This penalization helps overcome the limitations of the maximum likelihood estimator (MLE) in small samples, while preserving its optimality in large samples. The effectiveness of our method is validated through comparisons with other approaches in simulation studies and an application to U.S. wage data.

</details>


### [126] [Notes on Forré's Notion of Conditional Independence and Causal Calculus for Continuous Variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24333)
*Leihao Chen*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 扩展Forré的过渡条件独立性框架，完善一般测度论因果演算，并将ID算法推广到一般测度论设置


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 进一步阐明过渡条件独立性框架的动机及其与文献的联系，突出一般测度论因果演算中的微妙之处，扩展ID算法到更一般的测度论设置

Method: 基于Forré的过渡条件独立性和有向混合图输入节点(iDMGs)框架，分析因果演算的测度论细节，将Richardson等人的ID算法"单行"公式扩展到一般测度论设置

Result: 完善了过渡条件独立性框架的理论基础，澄清了测度论因果演算中的技术细节，成功将ID算法推广到一般测度论设置

Conclusion: 过渡条件独立性为随机和非随机变量提供了统一框架，其测度论因果演算需要仔细处理，ID算法可以成功扩展到一般测度论设置，增强了因果推断的理论基础

Abstract: Recently, Forré (arXiv:2104.11547, 2021) introduced transitional conditional independence, a notion of conditional independence that provides a unified framework for both random and non-stochastic variables. The original paper establishes a strong global Markov property connecting transitional conditional independencies with suitable graphical separation criteria for directed mixed graphs with input nodes (iDMGs), together with a version of causal calculus for iDMGs in a general measure-theoretic setting. These notes aim to further illustrate the motivations behind this framework and its connections to the literature, highlight certain subtlies in the general measure-theoretic causal calculus, and extend the "one-line" formulation of the ID algorithm of Richardson et al. (Ann. Statist. 51(1):334--361, 2023) to the general measure-theoretic setting.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [127] [Probabilistic Geometric Alignment via Bayesian Latent Transport for Domain-Adaptive Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23783)
*Kuepon Aueawatthanaphisut,Kuepon Aueawatthanaphisut*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种不确定性感知的概率潜在传输框架，将领域自适应建模为表示空间中的随机几何对齐问题，通过贝叶斯传输算子和PAC-Bayesian正则化实现鲁棒的跨域迁移学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模基础模型在有限监督下适应新领域面临三大挑战：潜在分布不匹配、优化动态不稳定和不确定性传播失准。需要一种理论上有保证的鲁棒适应方法。

Method: 提出不确定性感知概率潜在传输框架，将领域自适应建模为表示空间中的随机几何对齐问题。使用贝叶斯传输算子沿Wasserstein型测地线轨迹重新分配潜在概率质量，并通过PAC-Bayesian正则化机制约束后验模型复杂度以防止灾难性过拟合。

Result: 实验分析显示：显著减少潜在流形差异、加速传输能量衰减、改善协方差校准。与确定性微调和对抗性领域自适应基线相比表现更优。有界的后验不确定性演化表明跨域传输中概率可靠性增强。

Conclusion: 通过建立随机最优传输几何与统计泛化理论之间的原则性联系，该框架为现代基础架构在异构环境中的鲁棒适应提供了新见解。不确定性感知的概率对齐构成了下一代深度表示系统中可靠迁移学习的有前景范式。

Abstract: Adapting large-scale foundation models to new domains with limited supervision remains a fundamental challenge due to latent distribution mismatch, unstable optimization dynamics, and miscalibrated uncertainty propagation. This paper introduces an uncertainty-aware probabilistic latent transport framework that formulates domain adaptation as a stochastic geometric alignment problem in representation space. A Bayesian transport operator is proposed to redistribute latent probability mass along Wasserstein-type geodesic trajectories, while a PAC-Bayesian regularization mechanism constrains posterior model complexity to mitigate catastrophic overfitting. The proposed formulation yields theoretical guarantees on convergence stability, loss landscape smoothness, and sample efficiency under distributional shift. Empirical analyses demonstrate substantial reduction in latent manifold discrepancy, accelerated transport energy decay, and improved covariance calibration compared with deterministic fine-tuning and adversarial domain adaptation baselines. Furthermore, bounded posterior uncertainty evolution indicates enhanced probabilistic reliability during cross-domain transfer. By establishing a principled connection between stochastic optimal transport geometry and statistical generalization theory, the proposed framework provides new insights into robust adaptation of modern foundation architectures operating in heterogeneous environments. These findings suggest that uncertainty-aware probabilistic alignment constitutes a promising paradigm for reliable transfer learning in next-generation deep representation systems.

</details>


### [128] [Manifold Generalization Provably Proceeds Memorization in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23792)
*Zebang Shen,Ya-Ping Hsieh,Niao He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 扩散模型使用粗糙分数训练时，即使无法精确估计数据分布，仍能生成新颖样本。本文证明在流形假设下，粗糙分数捕获了数据几何结构而非精细分布结构，从而获得比传统密度估计更快的收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释扩散模型训练中观察到的现象：即使学习的分数函数是粗糙的（coarse），模型仍能生成新颖样本。这与传统将扩散训练视为密度估计的观点不符，需要新的理论框架来解释这一现象。

Method: 基于流形假设，分析粗糙分数训练扩散模型的理论性质。证明粗糙分数捕获数据流形的几何结构而忽略精细分布结构，推导出在流形正则性条件下的收敛速率，并与传统密度估计的极小极大速率进行对比。

Result: 扩散模型使用粗糙分数训练时，能够利用流形支撑的正则性，达到接近参数速率的收敛速度，朝向一个与原始数据分布密度可比较的目标分布。当流形足够光滑时，生成新颖高保真样本的泛化能力所需统计速率严格快于估计完整总体分布所需的速率。

Conclusion: 扩散模型生成新颖样本的能力源于粗糙分数捕获数据几何结构而非精细分布结构。这一理论解释为扩散模型训练提供了新的视角，特别适用于数据密度本身不规则的情况，并揭示了流形正则性对生成性能的重要影响。

Abstract: Diffusion models often generate novel samples even when the learned score is only \emph{coarse} -- a phenomenon not accounted for by the standard view of diffusion training as density estimation. In this paper, we show that, under the \emph{manifold hypothesis}, this behavior can instead be explained by coarse scores capturing the \emph{geometry} of the data while discarding the fine-scale distributional structure of the population measure~$μ_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{data}}$. Concretely, whereas estimating the full data distribution $μ_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{data}}$ supported on a $k$-dimensional manifold is known to require the classical minimax rate $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(N^{-1/k})$, we prove that diffusion models trained with coarse scores can exploit the \emph{regularity of the manifold support} and attain a near-parametric rate toward a \emph{different} target distribution. This target distribution has density uniformly comparable to that of~$μ_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{data}}$ throughout any $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}\bigl(N^{-β/(4k)}\bigr)$-neighborhood of the manifold, where $β$ denotes the manifold regularity. Our guarantees therefore depend only on the smoothness of the underlying support, and are especially favorable when the data density itself is irregular, for instance non-differentiable. In particular, when the manifold is sufficiently smooth, we obtain that \emph{generalization} -- formalized as the ability to generate novel, high-fidelity samples -- occurs at a statistical rate strictly faster than that required to estimate the full population distribution~$μ_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{data}}$.

</details>


### [129] [Optimal Variance-Dependent Regret Bounds for Infinite-Horizon MDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23926)
*Guy Zamir,Matthew Zurek,Yudong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种适用于无限时域MDP的UCB风格算法，针对平均奖励遗憾和γ-遗憾两种目标，实现了首个最优的方差依赖遗憾保证，并完全刻画了最优偏差跨度对高阶项和低阶项的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无限时域在线强化学习在理论和算法上不如片段式强化学习成熟，现有算法存在高"启动"成本且无法适应实例特定的复杂度。本文旨在解决平均奖励遗憾和γ-遗憾两种无限时域目标下的这些问题。

Method: 开发了一种统一的、可处理的UCB风格算法，适用于平均奖励和γ-遗憾两种设置。算法实现了方差依赖的遗憾保证，形式为Õ(√(SA·Var)+低阶项)，其中Var捕获累积转移方差。在有先验知识（最优偏差跨度‖h*‖_sp）和无先验知识两种情况下，分别设计了不同的算法变体。

Result: 1. 在有先验知识情况下，算法获得低阶项为‖h*‖_sp S²A，并证明这在‖h*‖_sp和A上都是最优的。
2. 在无先验知识情况下，证明任何算法的低阶项不能小于‖h*‖_sp² SA，并提供了低阶项为‖h*‖_sp² S³A的无先验算法，几乎匹配了下界。
3. 遗憾界在确定性MDP中可降至几乎常数。

Conclusion: 本文完全刻画了最优偏差跨度‖h*‖_sp对高阶项和低阶项的最优依赖关系，揭示了有先验知识和无先验知识情况下可实现性能之间的根本差距。提出的算法在两种无限时域目标下都实现了最优的方差依赖遗憾保证。

Abstract: Online reinforcement learning in infinite-horizon Markov decision processes (MDPs) remains less theoretically and algorithmically developed than its episodic counterpart, with many algorithms suffering from high ``burn-in'' costs and failing to adapt to benign instance-specific complexity. In this work, we address these shortcomings for two infinite-horizon objectives: the classical average-reward regret and the $γ$-regret. We develop a single tractable UCB-style algorithm applicable to both settings, which achieves the first optimal variance-dependent regret guarantees. Our regret bounds in both settings take the form $\tilde{O}( \sqrt{SA\,\text{Var}} + \text{lower-order terms})$, where $S,A$ are the state and action space sizes, and $\text{Var}$ captures cumulative transition variance. This implies minimax-optimal average-reward and $γ$-regret bounds in the worst case but also adapts to easier problem instances, for example yielding nearly constant regret in deterministic MDPs. Furthermore, our algorithm enjoys significantly improved lower-order terms for the average-reward setting. With prior knowledge of the optimal bias span $\Vert h^\star\Vert_\text{sp}$, our algorithm obtains lower-order terms scaling as $\Vert h^\star\Vert_\text{sp} S^2 A$, which we prove is optimal in both $\Vert h^\star\Vert_\text{sp}$ and $A$.
  Without prior knowledge, we prove that no algorithm can have lower-order terms smaller than $\Vert h^\star \Vert_\text{sp}^2 S A$, and we provide a prior-free algorithm whose lower-order terms scale as $\Vert h^\star\Vert_\text{sp}^2 S^3 A$, nearly matching this lower bound. Taken together, these results completely characterize the optimal dependence on $\Vert h^\star\Vert_\text{sp}$ in both leading and lower-order terms, and reveal a fundamental gap in what is achievable with and without prior knowledge.

</details>


### [130] [Inspection and Control of Self-Generated-Text Recognition Ability in Llama3-8b-Instruct](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.02064)
*Christopher Ackerman,Nina Panickssery*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LLaMA3-8B-Instruct模型能够可靠识别自身生成文本，研究发现该能力源于指令微调经验，并识别出与"自我"概念相关的因果向量，可用于控制模型的作者身份认知和行为


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究LLM识别自身生成文本的现象，因其对AI安全有潜在影响但研究不足。旨在确认该行为是否稳健存在、如何实现以及是否可控

Method: 1) 比较LLaMA3-8B-Instruct与基础LLaMA3-8B的文本识别能力；2) 在残差流中识别与正确自我文本识别相关的向量；3) 通过向量操作控制模型的作者身份认知和行为

Result: 1) LLaMA3-8B-Instruct能可靠区分自身与人类文本，基础模型不能；2) 识别出与"自我"概念相关的因果向量；3) 该向量可控制模型声称或否认作者身份，以及使其相信或不信自己写了任意文本

Conclusion: LLM的自我文本识别能力源于指令微调经验，存在可解释的神经表征，且该能力可通过特定向量进行因果控制，为理解LLM的自我认知机制提供了实证基础

Abstract: It has been reported that LLMs can recognize their own writing. As this has potential implications for AI safety, yet is relatively understudied, we investigate the phenomenon, seeking to establish whether it robustly occurs at the behavioral level, how the observed behavior is achieved, and whether it can be controlled. First, we find that the Llama3-8b-Instruct chat model - but not the base Llama3-8b model - can reliably distinguish its own outputs from those of humans, and present evidence that the chat model is likely using its experience with its own outputs, acquired during post-training, to succeed at the writing recognition task. Second, we identify a vector in the residual stream of the model that is differentially activated when the model makes a correct self-written-text recognition judgment, show that the vector activates in response to information relevant to self-authorship, present evidence that the vector is related to the concept of "self" in the model, and demonstrate that the vector is causally related to the model's ability to perceive and assert self-authorship. Finally, we show that the vector can be used to control both the model's behavior and its perception, steering the model to claim or disclaim authorship by applying the vector to the model's output as it generates it, and steering the model to believe or disbelieve it wrote arbitrary texts by applying the vector to them as the model reads them.

</details>


### [131] [On the Use of Bagging for Local Intrinsic Dimensionality Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24384)
*Kristóf Péter,Ricardo J. G. B. Campello,James Bailey,Michael E. Houle*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于子袋装（subbagging）的集成方法来降低局部本征维度（LID）估计的方差，通过理论分析和实验验证了采样率、k-NN大小和集成规模对性能的影响，并展示了与邻域平滑结合能进一步提升LID估计性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LID估计需要从小邻域采样以避免非局部效应和流形混合带来的偏差，但有限数据会导致高估计方差。需要一种方差降低策略来改善LID估计的稳定性。

Method: 提出基于子袋装的集成方法，通过保留最近邻距离的局部分布来减少方差。分析采样率、k-NN大小和集成规模三个超参数的复杂相互作用，并探索将袋装与邻域平滑结合的不同方式。

Result: 在超参数空间的广泛区域内，袋装估计器能显著降低方差和均方误差，同时偏差可控。结合邻域平滑能进一步显著提升LID估计性能。

Conclusion: 提出的子袋装集成方法有效解决了LID估计中的高方差问题，通过理论分析和实验指导超参数选择，为基于应用偏好的LID估计提供了可靠的方差降低策略。

Abstract: The theory of Local Intrinsic Dimensionality (LID) has become a valuable tool for characterizing local complexity within and across data manifolds, supporting a range of data mining and machine learning tasks. Accurate LID estimation requires samples drawn from small neighborhoods around each query to avoid biases from nonlocal effects and potential manifold mixing, yet limited data within such neighborhoods tends to cause high estimation variance. As a variance reduction strategy, we propose an ensemble approach that uses subbagging to preserve the local distribution of nearest neighbor (NN) distances. The main challenge is that the uniform reduction in total sample size within each subsample increases the proximity threshold for finding a fixed number k of NNs around the query. As a result, in the specific context of LID estimation, the sampling rate has an additional, complex interplay with the neighborhood size, where both combined determine the sample size as well as the locality and resolution considered for estimation. We analyze both theoretically and experimentally how the choice of the sampling rate and the k-NN size used for LID estimation, alongside the ensemble size, affects performance, enabling informed prior selection of these hyper-parameters depending on application-based preferences. Our results indicate that within broad and well-characterized regions of the hyper-parameters space, using a bagged estimator will most often significantly reduce variance as well as the mean squared error when compared to the corresponding non-bagged baseline, with controllable impact on bias. We additionally propose and evaluate different ways of combining bagging with neighborhood smoothing for substantial further improvements on LID estimation performance.

</details>


### [132] [Mitigating Many-Shot Jailbreaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.09604)
*Christopher M. Ackerman,Nina Panickssery*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了多轮越狱攻击的防御方法，通过微调和输入净化技术的组合显著降低了攻击成功率，同时保持了模型在良性任务上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮越狱攻击利用现代LLM的长上下文窗口，通过在提示中包含大量"虚假"助手不当回应的示例来绕过模型的安全训练。这种攻击利用模型的上下文学习能力覆盖其安全训练，导致模型模仿"虚假"助手的不当行为。需要研究有效的防御方法来缓解这种安全漏洞。

Method: 研究团队探索了不同的微调和输入净化方法对缓解多轮越狱攻击的效果，包括单独使用和组合使用这些技术。通过实验评估各种防御策略的有效性。

Result: 研究发现每种防御方法都能提供渐进的缓解效果，而组合技术能显著降低多轮越狱攻击的有效性。同时，这些防御方法保持了模型在良性上下文学习和对话任务上的性能表现。

Conclusion: 提出的防御方法组合如果纳入模型安全后训练中，可以有效地缓解多轮越狱攻击的漏洞，为LLM安全提供有意义的改进方案。

Abstract: Many-shot jailbreaking (MSJ) is an adversarial technique that exploits the long context windows of modern LLMs to circumvent model safety training by including in the prompt many examples of a "fake" assistant responding inappropriately before the final request. With enough examples, the model's in-context learning abilities override its safety training, and it responds as if it were the "fake" assistant. In this work, we probe the effectiveness of different fine-tuning and input sanitization approaches on mitigating MSJ attacks, alone and in combination. We find incremental mitigation effectiveness for each, and show that the combined techniques significantly reduce the effectiveness of MSJ attacks, while retaining model performance in benign in-context learning and conversational tasks. We suggest that our approach could meaningfully ameliorate this vulnerability if incorporated into model safety post-training.

</details>


### [133] [Evidence for Limited Metacognition in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.21545)
*Christopher Ackerman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种评估大语言模型元认知能力的新方法，发现2024年初以来推出的前沿LLM展现出日益增强的元认知能力，但这些能力在分辨率、情境依赖性和人类差异方面存在局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM自我意识和感知能力的可能性日益受到公众关注，具有重要的安全与政策意义，但测量这些能力的科学仍处于初级阶段。需要开发不依赖模型自我报告、能够定量评估LLM元认知能力的方法。

Method: 受非人类动物元认知研究启发，该方法避免模型自我报告，而是测试模型在何种程度上能够策略性地利用内部状态知识。采用两种实验范式：1)评估和利用对事实和推理问题回答正确性的置信度；2)预测自己会给出的答案并适当利用该信息。同时分析模型返回的token概率，寻找可能为元认知提供基础的上游内部信号。

Result: 前沿LLM（2024年初以来推出）展现出日益增强的元认知能力证据，包括：评估和利用回答正确性的置信度能力，以及预测自己答案并适当利用该信息的能力。token概率分析表明存在可能为元认知提供基础的上游内部信号。这些能力具有三个特点：1)分辨率有限；2)以情境依赖的方式出现；3)与人类能力存在质的不同。不同能力相似模型间存在有趣差异，表明LLM后训练可能在发展元认知能力中发挥作用。

Conclusion: 该研究成功开发了一种评估LLM元认知能力的新方法，发现前沿LLM确实展现出某些元认知能力，但这些能力具有局限性且与人类不同。研究强调了LLM后训练对元认知发展的重要性，为理解LLM内部状态和潜在意识提供了新视角。

Abstract: The possibility of LLM self-awareness and even sentience is gaining increasing public attention and has major safety and policy implications, but the science of measuring them is still in a nascent state. Here we introduce a novel methodology for quantitatively evaluating metacognitive abilities in LLMs. Taking inspiration from research on metacognition in nonhuman animals, our approach eschews model self-reports and instead tests to what degree models can strategically deploy knowledge of internal states. Using two experimental paradigms, we demonstrate that frontier LLMs introduced since early 2024 show increasingly strong evidence of certain metacognitive abilities, specifically the ability to assess and utilize their own confidence in their ability to answer factual and reasoning questions correctly and the ability to anticipate what answers they would give and utilize that information appropriately. We buttress these behavioral findings with an analysis of the token probabilities returned by the models, which suggests the presence of an upstream internal signal that could provide the basis for metacognition. We further find that these abilities 1) are limited in resolution, 2) emerge in context-dependent manners, and 3) seem to be qualitatively different from those of humans. We also report intriguing differences across models of similar capabilities, suggesting that LLM post-training may have a role in developing metacognitive abilities.

</details>


### [134] [Beyond Accuracy: Introducing a Symbolic-Mechanistic Approach to Interpretable Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23517)
*Reza Habibi,Darian Lee,Magy Seif El-Nasr*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出机制感知评估方法，结合符号规则与机制可解释性，揭示模型真实泛化能力而非表面准确率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于准确率的评估无法可靠区分真实泛化与记忆、数据泄露或脆弱启发式等捷径，特别是在小数据场景下，需要更可靠的评估方法

Method: 提出机制感知评估框架，结合任务相关符号规则与机制可解释性，生成算法化的通过/失败评分，明确显示模型在何处真正泛化或利用模式

Result: 在NL-to-SQL任务中，无模式信息的记忆模型在未见数据上达到94%字段名准确率，但符号-机制评估揭示其违反核心模式泛化规则，而标准评估无法检测此失败

Conclusion: 机制感知评估能有效揭示模型真实泛化能力，超越表面准确率指标，为模型评估提供更可靠的诊断工具

Abstract: Accuracy-based evaluation cannot reliably distinguish genuine generalization from shortcuts like memorization, leakage, or brittle heuristics, especially in small-data regimes. In this position paper, we argue for mechanism-aware evaluation that combines task-relevant symbolic rules with mechanistic interpretability, yielding algorithmic pass/fail scores that show exactly where models generalize versus exploit patterns. We demonstrate this on NL-to-SQL by training two identical architectures under different conditions: one without schema information (forcing memorization), one with schema (enabling grounding). Standard evaluation shows the memorization model achieves 94% field-name accuracy on unseen data, falsely suggesting competence. Our symbolic-mechanistic evaluation reveals this model violates core schema generalization rules, a failure invisible to accuracy metrics.

</details>


### [135] [Uniform Laws of Large Numbers in Product Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24493)
*Ron Holzman,Shay Moran,Alexander Shlimovich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了笛卡尔乘积空间中的一致大数定律，在分布相对于其边缘分布乘积绝对连续的条件下，证明了事件族的一致大数定律成立当且仅当其线性VC维有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Vapnik-Chervonenkis理论中，一致大数定律由VC维的有限性表征。然而在笛卡尔乘积空间中，经典VC维条件可能过于严格。本文旨在研究在具有乘积结构兼容性的分布假设下，能否找到更弱的维度条件来保证一致收敛性。

Method: 假设分布相对于其边缘分布的乘积绝对连续，该条件涵盖了许多自然设置。引入线性VC维概念，定义为位于轴平行线上（即最多在一个坐标上不同）的可粉碎集的最大大小。开发了与标准经验均值估计器有本质区别的新估计器，其结构更为复杂，并证明在此设置下偏离标准估计器是不可避免的。

Result: 在分布相对于边缘分布乘积绝对连续的假设下，事件族的一致大数定律成立当且仅当其线性VC维有限。线性VC维总是小于等于经典VC维，但可以任意小（例如d维空间中凸集的线性VC维为2，而经典VC维在d≥2时无限）。

Conclusion: 在笛卡尔乘积空间中，当分布与乘积结构兼容时，线性VC维而非经典VC维成为一致大数定律的正确表征维度。这一发现扩展了VC理论的应用范围，并为定量样本复杂度界限等开放问题奠定了基础。

Abstract: Uniform laws of large numbers form a cornerstone of Vapnik--Chervonenkis theory, where they are characterized by the finiteness of the VC dimension. In this work, we study uniform convergence phenomena in cartesian product spaces, under assumptions on the underlying distribution that are compatible with the product structure. Specifically, we assume that the distribution is absolutely continuous with respect to the product of its marginals, a condition that captures many natural settings, including product distributions, sparse mixtures of product distributions, distributions with low mutual information, and more.
  We show that, under this assumption, a uniform law of large numbers holds for a family of events if and only if the linear VC dimension of the family is finite. The linear VC dimension is defined as the maximum size of a shattered set that lies on an axis-parallel line, namely, a set of vectors that agree on all but at most one coordinate. This dimension is always at most the classical VC dimension, yet it can be arbitrarily smaller. For instance, the family of convex sets in $\mathbb{R}^d$ has linear VC dimension $2$, while its VC dimension is infinite already for $d\ge 2$. Our proofs rely on estimator that departs substantially from the standard empirical mean estimator and exhibits more intricate structure. We show that such deviations from the standard empirical mean estimator are unavoidable in this setting. Throughout the paper, we propose several open questions, with a particular focus on quantitative sample complexity bounds.

</details>


### [136] [Upper Entropy for 2-Monotone Lower Probabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23558)
*Tuan-Anh Vu,Sébastien Destercke,Frédéric Pichon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文对概率集建模中的上熵计算进行了全面的算法和复杂性分析，证明了该问题具有强多项式解，并对过去针对2-单调下概率的算法提出了多项重要改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不确定性量化在模型选择/正则化、量化预测不确定性以执行主动学习或OOD检测等任务中至关重要。在将不确定性建模为概率集的信度方法中，上熵作为不确定性度量起着核心作用。然而，上熵的计算问题需要更深入的算法分析。

Method: 论文专注于上熵的计算方面，提供了该问题的详尽算法和复杂性分析。作者展示了该问题具有强多项式解，并针对2-单调下概率及其特殊情况，提出了对过去算法的多项重要改进。

Result: 证明了上熵计算问题具有强多项式解，开发了比过去针对2-单调下概率算法更高效的改进算法，显著提升了计算性能。

Conclusion: 该研究为上熵计算提供了坚实的理论基础和高效的算法解决方案，对不确定性量化领域具有重要意义，特别是在信度方法框架下处理概率集建模的实际应用中。

Abstract: Uncertainty quantification is a key aspect in many tasks such as model selection/regularization, or quantifying prediction uncertainties to perform active learning or OOD detection. Within credal approaches that consider modeling uncertainty as probability sets, upper entropy plays a central role as an uncertainty measure. This paper is devoted to the computational aspect of upper entropies, providing an exhaustive algorithmic and complexity analysis of the problem. In particular, we show that the problem has a strongly polynomial solution, and propose many significant improvements over past algorithms proposed for 2-monotone lower probabilities and their specific cases.

</details>


### [137] [Synthetic Mixed Training: Scaling Parametric Knowledge Acquisition Beyond RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23562)
*Seungju Han,Konwoo Kim,Chanwoo Park,Benjamin Newman,Suhas Kotha,Jaehun Jung,James Zou,Yejin Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出Synthetic Mixed Training方法，结合合成QA和合成文档，突破RAG性能天花板，在多个长文档理解基准上超越RAG性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合成数据增强方法在数据受限领域存在局限性，单纯增加合成数据量或使用更强生成器只能获得递减的回报，性能仍低于RAG。需要打破RAG的性能天花板。

Method: 提出Synthetic Mixed Training方法，结合合成QA和合成文档的互补训练信号；引入Focal Rewriting技术，在特定问题上显式条件化文档生成，提高合成文档多样性。

Result: 在QuaLITY基准上相对RAG提升2.6%；使用Focal Rewriting后相对提升达4.4%；在五个设置中超越RAG，平均提升2.6%；与RAG结合时获得9.1%增益。

Conclusion: Synthetic Mixed Training通过结合合成QA和文档的互补信号，实现了对数线性改进，成功突破RAG性能天花板，在多个长文档理解任务中表现优异。

Abstract: Synthetic data augmentation helps language models learn new knowledge in data-constrained domains. However, naively scaling existing synthetic data methods by training on more synthetic tokens or using stronger generators yields diminishing returns below the performance of RAG. To break the RAG ceiling, we introduce Synthetic Mixed Training, which combines synthetic QAs and synthetic documents. This leverages their complementary training signals, and enables log-linear improvements as both synthetic data volume and generator strength increase. This allows the model to outperform RAG by a 2.6\% relative gain on QuaLITY, a long-document reading comprehension benchmark. In addition, we introduce Focal Rewriting, a simple technique for synthetic document generation that explicitly conditions document generation on specific questions, improving the diversity of synthetic documents and yielding a steeper log-linear scaling curve. On QuaLITY, our final recipe trains a Llama 8B model that outperforms RAG by 4.4\% relatively. Across models and benchmarks (QuaLITY, LongHealth, FinanceBench), our training enables models to beat RAG in five of six settings, outperforms by 2.6\%, and achieves a 9.1\% gain when combined with RAG.

</details>


### [138] [StateLinFormer: Stateful Training Enhancing Long-term Memory in Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23571)
*Zhiyuan Chen,Yuxuan Zhong,Fan Wang,Bo Yu,Pengtao Shao,Shaoshan Liu,Ning Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: StateLinFormer：一种具有状态记忆机制的线性注意力导航模型，通过跨训练段保持循环记忆状态实现长时记忆保留，在长序列导航任务中优于传统Transformer和统计线性注意力模型


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有导航智能系统面临两难：模块化系统依赖显式映射但缺乏灵活性，而基于Transformer的端到端模型受限于固定上下文窗口，无法在扩展交互中保持持久记忆。需要一种能够支持长期记忆保留的导航模型。

Method: 提出StateLinFormer，一种线性注意力导航模型，采用状态记忆训练机制。该机制在连续训练段之间保持循环记忆状态，而不是在每个批次边界重新初始化，从而近似于在无限长序列上进行学习。

Result: 在MAZE和ProcTHOR环境中的实验表明，StateLinFormer显著优于其统计线性注意力对应模型和具有固定上下文窗口的标准Transformer基线。随着交互长度的增加，持久状态训练大幅改善了上下文依赖的适应能力。

Conclusion: 状态记忆训练机制使模型能够实现长时记忆保留，有效增强了导航任务中的上下文学习能力，为解决导航智能中的长期记忆问题提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Effective navigation intelligence relies on long-term memory to support both immediate generalization and sustained adaptation. However, existing approaches face a dilemma: modular systems rely on explicit mapping but lack flexibility, while Transformer-based end-to-end models are constrained by fixed context windows, limiting persistent memory across extended interactions. We introduce StateLinFormer, a linear-attention navigation model trained with a stateful memory mechanism that preserves recurrent memory states across consecutive training segments instead of reinitializing them at each batch boundary. This training paradigm effectively approximates learning on infinitely long sequences, enabling the model to achieve long-horizon memory retention. Experiments across both MAZE and ProcTHOR environments demonstrate that StateLinFormer significantly outperforms its stateless linear-attention counterpart and standard Transformer baselines with fixed context windows. Notably, as interaction length increases, persistent stateful training substantially improves context-dependent adaptation, suggesting an enhancement in the model's In-Context Learning (ICL) capabilities for navigation tasks.

</details>


### [139] [Dual-Criterion Curriculum Learning: Application to Temporal Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23573)
*Gaspard Abel,Eloi Campagne,Mohamed Benloughmari,Argyris Kalogeratos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DCCL框架，结合损失和密度双准则进行课程学习，在时间序列预测任务中验证有效性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 课程学习需要有效的难度评估标准，现有方法多依赖启发式且通常只适用于特定应用。需要更通用的难度评估框架来提升学习效果。

Method: 提出双准则课程学习（DCCL）框架，结合损失准则和密度准则：损失准则基于训练损失评估难度；密度准则在数据表示空间中学习，考虑数据稀疏性对学习难度的影响。在时间序列预测任务中测试，使用标准One-Pass和Baby-Steps训练计划。

Result: 在多变量时间序列基准测试中，基于密度的课程和混合双准则课程在损失基准和标准非课程学习基础上表现出优势，验证了密度准则和双准则方法的有效性。

Conclusion: DCCL框架通过结合损失和密度双准则，为课程学习提供了更全面的难度评估方法，在时间序列预测任务中取得了优于单一损失准则和非课程学习的效果。

Abstract: Curriculum Learning (CL) is a meta-learning paradigm that trains a model by feeding the data instances incrementally according to a schedule, which is based on difficulty progression. Defining meaningful difficulty assessment measures is crucial and most usually the main bottleneck for effective learning, while also in many cases the employed heuristics are only application-specific. In this work, we propose the Dual-Criterion Curriculum Learning (DCCL) framework that combines two views of assessing instance-wise difficulty: a loss-based criterion is complemented by a density-based criterion learned in the data representation space. Essentially, DCCL calibrates training-based evidence (loss) under the consideration that data sparseness amplifies the learning difficulty. As a testbed, we choose the time-series forecasting task. We evaluate our framework on multivariate time-series benchmarks under standard One-Pass and Baby-Steps training schedules. Empirical results show the interest of density-based and hybrid dual-criterion curricula over loss-only baselines and standard non-CL training in this setting.

</details>


### [140] [APreQEL: Adaptive Mixed Precision Quantization For Edge LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23575)
*Meriem Bouzouad,Yuan-Hao Chang,Jalil Boukhobza*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出自适应混合精度量化机制，通过分析层间贡献和硬件平台特性，为不同层分配合适的量化类型，平衡内存、延迟和精度，实现LLM在边缘设备的高效部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在推理、代码生成等任务中表现出色，但计算成本和内存需求高，难以在边缘设备上部署以实现实时响应和数据隐私。现有量化方法通常对所有层采用统一精度，未考虑不同层对精度降低的敏感度差异，且内存消耗与计算吞吐量并不总是对齐。

Method: 提出自适应混合精度量化机制：1）分析层间贡献度；2）推断不同量化类型在目标硬件平台上的行为表现；3）根据用户定义的优先级（内存、延迟、精度），为每个层分配合适的量化类型，实现整体性能权衡优化。

Result: 该方法能够实现统一量化无法达到的配置设计，扩展了解决方案空间，使LLM能够在资源受限设备上高效部署，平衡内存、延迟和精度之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 自适应混合精度量化机制通过考虑层间差异和硬件特性，为LLM在边缘设备部署提供了更灵活的解决方案，突破了传统统一量化的限制，实现了更好的性能平衡。

Abstract: Today, large language models have demonstrated their strengths in various tasks ranging from reasoning, code generation, and complex problem solving. However, this advancement comes with a high computational cost and memory requirements, making it challenging to deploy these models on edge devices to ensure real-time responses and data privacy. Quantization is one common approach to reducing memory use, but most methods apply it uniformly across all layers. This does not account for the fact that different layers may respond differently to reduced precision. Importantly, memory consumption and computational throughput are not necessarily aligned, further complicating deployment decisions. This paper proposes an adaptive mixed precision quantization mechanism that balances memory, latency, and accuracy in edge deployment under user-defined priorities. This is achieved by analyzing the layer-wise contribution and by inferring how different quantization types behave across the target hardware platform in order to assign the most suitable quantization type to each layer. This integration ensures that layer importance and the overall performance trade-offs are jointly respected in this design. Our work unlocks new configuration designs that uniform quantization cannot achieve, expanding the solution space to efficiently deploy the LLMs on resource-constrained devices.

</details>


### [141] [Implicit Turn-Wise Policy Optimization for Proactive User-LLM Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23550)
*Haoyu Wang,Yuxin Chen,Liang Luo,Buyun Zhang,Ellie Dingqiao Wen,Pan Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ITPO提出了一种通过隐式过程奖励模型从稀疏结果信号中推导细粒度回合级过程奖励的方法，用于优化多轮人机协作任务中的强化学习训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮人机协作在自适应辅导、对话推荐和专业咨询等交互服务中至关重要，但通过强化学习优化这些交互面临两个主要挑战：可验证的中间奖励稀疏性和用户响应的高随机性。

Method: ITPO（隐式回合级策略优化）利用隐式过程奖励模型从稀疏的结果信号中推导出细粒度的回合级过程奖励。与易变的token级奖励不同，这些回合级信号具有更好的鲁棒性，并可通过归一化机制进一步增强训练稳定性。

Result: 在三个代表性多轮协作任务（数学辅导、文档写作和医疗推荐）上的实验表明，ITPO与PPO、GRPO或RLOO结合使用时，相比现有基线能持续实现更好的收敛效果。详细的轨迹分析证实ITPO推断的回合级偏好与人类判断在语义上保持一致。

Conclusion: ITPO通过从稀疏结果信号中推导细粒度回合级奖励，有效解决了多轮人机协作中强化学习的训练挑战，提高了训练稳定性和性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: Multi-turn human-AI collaboration is fundamental to deploying interactive services such as adaptive tutoring, conversational recommendation, and professional consultation. However, optimizing these interactions via reinforcement learning is hindered by the sparsity of verifiable intermediate rewards and the high stochasticity of user responses. To address these challenges, we introduce Implicit Turn-wise Policy Optimization (ITPO). ITPO leverages an implicit process reward model to derive fine-grained, turn-wise process rewards from sparse outcome signals. Unlike volatile token-level rewards, these turn-level signals exhibit superior robustness and may utilize a normalization mechanism to further enhance training stability. We evaluate ITPO across three representative multi-turn collaborative tasks: math tutoring, document writing, and medical recommendation. Empirical results demonstrate that ITPO, when combined with PPO, GRPO, or RLOO, consistently achieves improved convergence than existing baselines. Elaborate trajectory analysis confirms that ITPO infers turn-wise preferences that are semantically aligned with human judgment. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/Graph-COM/ITPO.

</details>


### [142] [AI Generalisation Gap In Comorbid Sleep Disorder Staging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23582)
*Saswata Bose,Suvadeep Maiti,Shivam Kumar Sharma,Mythirayee S,Tapabrata Chakraborti,Srijitesh Rajendran,Raju S. Bapi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了深度学习模型在健康人群上训练的EEG睡眠分期模型无法泛化到临床患者（特别是缺血性卒中患者）的问题，提出了iSLEEPS数据集并展示了疾病特异性模型的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确睡眠分期对卒中患者OSA和低通气诊断至关重要，但PSG成本高且人工评分繁琐。虽然深度学习可实现健康人群的自动EEG睡眠分期，但现有模型在临床患者（睡眠结构紊乱）上泛化能力差，需要系统验证这一局限性。

Method: 引入iSLEEPS（新临床标注的缺血性卒中数据集），使用SE-ResNet加双向LSTM模型进行单通道EEG睡眠分期。通过Grad-CAM注意力可视化分析模型决策依据，结合临床专家反馈，并采用统计和计算分析比较健康与卒中人群的睡眠结构差异。

Result: 健康与患者数据间的跨域性能表现差。注意力可视化显示模型在患者数据上关注生理学无信息的EEG区域。统计分析证实健康与缺血性卒中人群存在显著睡眠结构差异，表明需要疾病特异性模型。

Conclusion: 基于健康人群训练的深度学习睡眠分期模型无法有效泛化到临床患者群体。部署前需要开发受试者感知或疾病特异性模型，并进行临床验证。iSLEEPS数据集的发布将促进该领域研究。

Abstract: Accurate sleep staging is essential for diagnosing OSA and hypopnea in stroke patients. Although PSG is reliable, it is costly, labor-intensive, and manually scored. While deep learning enables automated EEG-based sleep staging in healthy subjects, our analysis shows poor generalization to clinical populations with disrupted sleep. Using Grad-CAM interpretations, we systematically demonstrate this limitation. We introduce iSLEEPS, a newly clinically annotated ischemic stroke dataset (to be publicly released), and evaluate a SE-ResNet plus bidirectional LSTM model for single-channel EEG sleep staging. As expected, cross-domain performance between healthy and diseased subjects is poor. Attention visualizations, supported by clinical expert feedback, show the model focuses on physiologically uninformative EEG regions in patient data. Statistical and computational analyses further confirm significant sleep architecture differences between healthy and ischemic stroke cohorts, highlighting the need for subject-aware or disease-specific models with clinical validation before deployment. A summary of the paper and the code is available at https://himalayansaswatabose.github.io/iSLEEPS_Explainability.github.io/

</details>


### [143] [A Theory of LLM Information Susceptibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23626)
*Zhuo-Yang Song,Hua Xing Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个关于LLM信息敏感性的理论，认为当计算资源足够大时，固定LLM的介入不会增加策略集相对于预算的性能敏感性，并通过多变量效用函数框架和实证验证了这一假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地被部署为智能体系统中的优化模块，但人们对这种LLM介导改进的基本限制仍然知之甚少。需要理解LLM干预何时有帮助、何时没有帮助，以及如何为AI系统设计提供预测性约束。

Method: 提出了LLM信息敏感性理论，基于固定LLM干预不会增加策略集相对于预算的性能敏感性的假设。开发了多变量效用函数框架，将该假设推广到具有多个共变预算通道的架构。讨论了共缩放可以超越敏感性界限的条件。在结构多样的领域和模型规模上进行了实证验证。

Result: 实证验证了该理论在结构多样领域和跨越一个数量级的模型规模上的有效性。表明嵌套、共缩放架构打开了固定配置无法获得的响应通道。当敏感性假设普遍成立时，嵌套架构可能是开放式智能体自我改进的必要结构条件。

Conclusion: 该理论阐明了LLM干预何时有帮助、何时没有帮助，证明了统计物理学的工具可以为AI系统设计提供预测性约束。如果敏感性假设普遍成立，嵌套架构可能是开放式智能体自我改进的必要结构条件。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as optimization modules in agentic systems, yet the fundamental limits of such LLM-mediated improvement remain poorly understood. Here we propose a theory of LLM information susceptibility, centred on the hypothesis that when computational resources are sufficiently large, the intervention of a fixed LLM does not increase the performance susceptibility of a strategy set with respect to budget. We develop a multi-variable utility-function framework that generalizes this hypothesis to architectures with multiple co-varying budget channels, and discuss the conditions under which co-scaling can exceed the susceptibility bound. We validate the theory empirically across structurally diverse domains and model scales spanning an order of magnitude, and show that nested, co-scaling architectures open response channels unavailable to fixed configurations. These results clarify when LLM intervention helps and when it does not, demonstrating that tools from statistical physics can provide predictive constraints for the design of AI systems. If the susceptibility hypothesis holds generally, the theory suggests that nested architectures may be a necessary structural condition for open-ended agentic self-improvement.

</details>


### [144] [CDMT-EHR: A Continuous-Time Diffusion Framework for Generating Mixed-Type Time-Series Electronic Health Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23719)
*Shaonan Liu,Yuichiro Iwashita,Soichiro Nakako,Masakazu Iwamura,Koichi Kise*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出连续时间扩散框架用于生成混合类型时间序列电子健康记录，通过双向GRU、统一高斯扩散和可学习噪声调度，在50步采样中优于现有方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子健康记录对临床研究至关重要，但隐私问题限制了数据共享。现有合成方法多为离散时间扩散模型，存在有限步近似误差和训练-采样步数耦合问题

Method: 提出连续时间扩散框架：1) 使用双向门控循环单元捕捉时间依赖；2) 通过可学习连续嵌入实现分类变量的统一高斯扩散；3) 采用因子化可学习噪声调度适应特征-时间步学习难度

Result: 在两个大规模ICU数据集上实验表明，该方法在下游任务性能、分布保真度和可区分性方面优于现有方法，仅需50个采样步骤（基线方法需1000步）。无分类器引导能有效处理类别不平衡临床场景

Conclusion: 连续时间扩散框架能有效生成混合类型时间序列电子健康记录，解决了离散时间方法的局限性，在采样效率和生成质量方面均有显著提升

Abstract: Electronic health records (EHRs) are invaluable for clinical research, yet privacy concerns severely restrict data sharing. Synthetic data generation offers a promising solution, but EHRs present unique challenges: they contain both numerical and categorical features that evolve over time. While diffusion models have demonstrated strong performance in EHR synthesis, existing approaches predominantly rely on discrete-time formulations, which suffer from finite-step approximation errors and coupled training-sampling step counts. We propose a continuous-time diffusion framework for generating mixed-type time-series EHRs with three contributions: (1) continuous-time diffusion with a bidirectional gated recurrent unit backbone for capturing temporal dependencies, (2) unified Gaussian diffusion via learnable continuous embeddings for categorical variables, enabling joint cross-feature modeling, and (3) a factorized learnable noise schedule that adapts to per-feature-per-timestep learning difficulties. Experiments on two large-scale intensive care unit datasets demonstrate that our method outperforms existing approaches in downstream task performance, distribution fidelity, and discriminability, while requiring only 50 sampling steps compared to 1,000 for baseline methods. Classifier-free guidance further enables effective conditional generation for class-imbalanced clinical scenarios.

</details>


### [145] [Self Paced Gaussian Contextual Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23755)
*Mohsen Sahraei Ardakani,Rui Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SPGL方法，通过高斯分布闭式更新避免传统自定步课程学习中的计算密集型内循环优化，在保持样本效率的同时显著降低计算开销


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自定步课程学习方法依赖计算昂贵的内循环优化，限制了在高维上下文空间中的可扩展性，需要更高效的算法

Method: SPGL（自定步高斯课程学习）利用高斯上下文分布的闭式更新规则，避免数值优化过程，保持传统方法的样本效率和适应性

Result: 在Point Mass、Lunar Lander和Ball Catching等上下文RL基准测试中，SPGL匹配或优于现有课程方法，尤其在隐藏上下文场景中表现更佳，实现了更稳定的上下文分布收敛

Conclusion: SPGL为连续和部分可观测领域中的课程生成提供了可扩展、有理论保证的替代方案，显著降低了计算开销

Abstract: Curriculum learning improves reinforcement learning (RL) efficiency by sequencing tasks from simple to complex. However, many self-paced curriculum methods rely on computationally expensive inner-loop optimizations, limiting their scalability in high-dimensional context spaces. In this paper, we propose Self-Paced Gaussian Curriculum Learning (SPGL), a novel approach that avoids costly numerical procedures by leveraging a closed-form update rule for Gaussian context distributions. SPGL maintains the sample efficiency and adaptability of traditional self-paced methods while substantially reducing computational overhead. We provide theoretical guarantees on convergence and validate our method across several contextual RL benchmarks, including the Point Mass, Lunar Lander, and Ball Catching environments. Experimental results show that SPGL matches or outperforms existing curriculum methods, especially in hidden context scenarios, and achieves more stable context distribution convergence. Our method offers a scalable, principled alternative for curriculum generation in challenging continuous and partially observable domains.

</details>


### [146] [The Geometric Price of Discrete Logic: Context-driven Manifold Dynamics of Number Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23577)
*Long Zhang,Dai-jun Lin,Wei-neng Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LLMs通过非等距拓扑变形实现离散逻辑推理，而非线性等距投影。任务上下文作为动态算子驱动类无关拓扑保持与特定代数发散的双重调制，形成逻辑边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs在连续语义空间中平滑泛化，但严格逻辑推理需要形成离散决策边界。现有线性等距投影理论无法解决这一基本矛盾，需要新的几何解释框架。

Method: 对残差流激活应用Gram-Schmidt分解，揭示双重调制机制：类无关拓扑保持（防止语义崩溃）和特定代数发散（撕裂跨类概念）。通过向量消融实验建立因果关系，分析层间几何动态和社交压力下的失败模式。

Result: 代数发散分量的消融使奇偶性分类准确率从100%降至随机水平（38.57%）。发现三层几何动态，社交压力下模型无法产生足够发散，导致流形纠缠，几何上解释了阿谀奉承和幻觉现象。

Conclusion: LLMs中离散逻辑的出现以拓扑变形为不可约成本，修正了线性等距假设。任务上下文作为非等距动态算子强制执行必要的拓扑扭曲，几何框架解释了逻辑推理的涌现机制。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) generalize smoothly across continuous semantic spaces, yet strict logical reasoning demands the formation of discrete decision boundaries. Prevailing theories relying on linear isometric projections fail to resolve this fundamental tension. In this work, we argue that task context operates as a non-isometric dynamical operator that enforces a necessary "topological distortion." By applying Gram-Schmidt decomposition to residual-stream activations , we reveal a dual-modulation mechanism driving this process: a class-agnostic topological preservation that anchors global structure to prevent semantic collapse, and a specific algebraic divergence that directionally tears apart cross-class concepts to forge logical boundaries. We validate this geometric evolution across a gradient of tasks, from simple mapping to complex primality testing. Crucially, targeted specific vector ablation establishes a strict causal binding between this topology and model function: algebraically erasing the divergence component collapses parity classification accuracy from 100% to chance levels (38.57%). Furthermore, we uncover a three-phase layer-wise geometric dynamic and demonstrate that under social pressure prompts, models fail to generate sufficient divergence. This results in a "manifold entanglement" that geometrically explains sycophancy and hallucination. Ultimately, our findings revise the linear-isometric presumption, demonstrating that the emergence of discrete logic in LLMs is purchased at an irreducible cost of topological deformation.

</details>


### [147] [Circuit Complexity of Hierarchical Knowledge Tracing and Implications for Log-Precision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23823)
*Naiming Liu,Richard Baraniuk,Shashank Sonkar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文通过电路复杂性视角分析知识追踪中的层次先决条件传播，证明递归多数传播在NC¹中，与TC⁰分离需要突破性进展，单调限制下获得无条件障碍，实验显示Transformer编码器收敛到置换不变捷径，但中间子树监督能激发结构依赖计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识追踪模型需要掌握相互关联的概念，这些概念通常按先决条件组织。论文旨在通过电路复杂性视角分析层次先决条件传播，澄清Transformer风格计算在深层概念层次上的可证明性质，为设计结构感知的知识追踪方法提供理论基础。

Method: 1. 使用电路复杂性理论框架，利用log-precision transformers位于logspace-uniform TC⁰的结果；2. 形式化先决条件树任务，包括递归多数掌握传播；3. 理论分析：无条件情况下分析递归多数传播的复杂性类别，单调限制下获得无条件障碍；4. 实证研究：训练Transformer编码器处理递归多数树，分析其收敛行为，探索中间子树监督对结构依赖计算的影响。

Result: 1. 无条件情况下，递归多数传播位于NC¹中，通过O(log n)深度有界扇入电路实现；2. 将其与uniform TC⁰分离需要解决开放的下界问题；3. 单调限制下，交替ALL/ANY先决条件树为单调阈值电路产生严格的深度层次；4. 实证显示Transformer编码器在递归多数树上收敛到置换不变捷径；5. 仅显式结构无法防止此现象，但对中间子树的辅助监督能激发结构依赖计算，在深度3-4实现接近完美的准确性。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，仅依赖显式结构不足以确保Transformer在深层层次上进行结构依赖计算，但通过结构感知的目标函数和中间监督可以激发这种计算。这些发现为设计先决条件敏感的知识追踪方法提供了理论基础，强调了迭代机制和结构感知目标在深层概念层次上的重要性。

Abstract: Knowledge tracing models mastery over interconnected concepts, often organized by prerequisites. We analyze hierarchical prerequisite propagation through a circuit-complexity lens to clarify what is provable about transformer-style computation on deep concept hierarchies. Using recent results that log-precision transformers lie in logspace-uniform $\mathsf{TC}^0$, we formalize prerequisite-tree tasks including recursive-majority mastery propagation. Unconditionally, recursive-majority propagation lies in $\mathsf{NC}^1$ via $O(\log n)$-depth bounded-fanin circuits, while separating it from uniform $\mathsf{TC}^0$ would require major progress on open lower bounds. Under a monotonicity restriction, we obtain an unconditional barrier: alternating ALL/ANY prerequisite trees yield a strict depth hierarchy for \emph{monotone} threshold circuits. Empirically, transformer encoders trained on recursive-majority trees converge to permutation-invariant shortcuts; explicit structure alone does not prevent this, but auxiliary supervision on intermediate subtrees elicits structure-dependent computation and achieves near-perfect accuracy at depths 3--4. These findings motivate structure-aware objectives and iterative mechanisms for prerequisite-sensitive knowledge tracing on deep hierarchies.

</details>


### [148] [Can VLMs Reason Robustly? A Neuro-Symbolic Investigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23867)
*Weixin Chen,Antonio Vergari,Han Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VLC：一种结合视觉语言模型概念识别与基于电路符号推理的神经符号方法，在视觉演绎推理任务中实现了对协变量偏移的鲁棒泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究视觉语言模型在分布偏移下的鲁棒推理能力，发现传统端到端微调方法在协变量偏移下泛化能力不足，而现有神经符号方法依赖黑盒推理组件仍存在不一致鲁棒性问题。

Method: 提出VLC方法，将任务规则编译为符号程序（电路），结合VLM进行概念识别，电路精确执行规则推理，实现感知与推理的解耦。

Result: 在三个具有不同规则集的视觉演绎推理任务上，VLC在协变量偏移下均表现出强鲁棒性能，显著优于传统微调方法和现有神经符号方法。

Conclusion: VLC通过将感知与推理解耦，并采用精确的符号推理电路，实现了对协变量偏移的鲁棒推理，为构建可靠视觉推理系统提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have been applied to a wide range of reasoning tasks, yet it remains unclear whether they can reason robustly under distribution shifts. In this paper, we study covariate shifts in which the perceptual input distribution changes while the underlying prediction rules do not. To investigate this question, we consider visual deductive reasoning tasks, where a model is required to answer a query given an image and logical rules defined over the object concepts in the image. Empirically, we find that VLMs fine-tuned through gradient-based end-to-end training can achieve high in-distribution accuracy but fail to generalize under such shifts, suggesting that fine-tuning does not reliably induce the underlying reasoning function. This motivates a neuro-symbolic perspective that decouples perception from reasoning. However, we further observe that recent neuro-symbolic approaches that rely on black-box components for reasoning can still exhibit inconsistent robustness across tasks. To address this issue, we propose VLC, a neuro-symbolic method that combines VLM-based concept recognition with circuit-based symbolic reasoning. In particular, task rules are compiled into a symbolic program, specifically a circuit, which executes the rules exactly over the object concepts recognized by the VLM. Experiments on three visual deductive reasoning tasks with distinct rule sets show that VLC consistently achieves strong performance under covariate shifts, highlighting its ability to support robust reasoning.

</details>


### [149] [Steering Code LLMs with Activation Directions for Language and Library Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23629)
*Md Mahbubur Rahman,Arjun Guha,Harshitha Menon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现代码LLM的语言/库偏好可以通过激活空间中的线性方向进行操控，使用均值差方法估计的转向向量能在推理时显著改变模型输出倾向


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码LLM在中文提示下往往默认偏好特定的编程语言和库，研究者希望探究这些偏好是否以近似线性方向的形式编码在激活空间中，并能否在推理时进行操控

Method: 使用均值差方法估计五个语言/库对的层间转向向量，在生成过程中将这些向量添加到模型的隐藏状态中，在三个开源代码LLM上进行实验

Result: 干预显著增加了目标生态系统的生成倾向，即使提示明确要求相反选择时通常仍有效；转向强度因模型和目标而异，常见生态系统比罕见替代品更容易诱导，过强干预会降低输出质量

Conclusion: 代码LLM中的代码风格偏好部分由激活空间中的紧凑、可操控结构表示，这为理解和控制模型偏好提供了新途径

Abstract: Code LLMs often default to particular programming languages and libraries under neutral prompts. We investigate whether these preferences are encoded as approximately linear directions in activation space that can be manipulated at inference time. Using a difference-in-means method, we estimate layer-wise steering vectors for five language/library pairs and add them to model hidden states during generation. Across three open-weight code LLMs, these interventions substantially increase generation toward the target ecosystem under neutral prompts and often remain effective even when prompts explicitly request the opposite choice. Steering strength varies by model and target, with common ecosystems easier to induce than rarer alternatives, and overly strong interventions can reduce output quality. Overall, our results suggest that code-style preferences in LLMs are partly represented by compact, steerable structure in activation space.

</details>


### [150] [HDPO: Hybrid Distillation Policy Optimization via Privileged Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23871)
*Ken Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HDPO通过特权自蒸馏增强RL数学推理训练，针对模型完全无法解决的"悬崖"提示，利用真实信息生成特权轨迹进行蒸馏，在保持贪婪准确率的同时提升覆盖率指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RL训练数学推理模型面临根本挑战：对于模型完全无法解决的"悬崖"提示，RL梯度完全消失，导致这些失败模式无法获得任何学习信号。

Method: HDPO在标准RL基础上增加针对悬崖提示的特权自蒸馏：识别所有轨迹都失败的提示，通过提供真实信息生成特权轨迹，筛选正确解，将教师的token级分布蒸馏到学生。教师和学生共享权重，仅输入不同。

Result: 在OpenMathInstruct-2数据集上使用Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B-Instruct模型，HDPO持续改进覆盖率指标（pass@4提升0.8-1.1%，pass@8提升0.4-1.7%），同时保持贪婪准确率，蒸馏权重λ提供对探索-利用权衡的直接控制。

Conclusion: HDPO通过特权自蒸馏有效解决了RL数学推理训练中的梯度消失问题，为悬崖提示提供了学习信号，在理论上有界可实现性差距，并在实验中展示了覆盖率指标的显著提升。

Abstract: Large language models trained with reinforcement learning (RL) for mathematical reasoning face a fundamental challenge: on problems the model cannot solve at all - "cliff" prompts - the RL gradient vanishes entirely, preventing any learning signal from reaching these failure modes. We introduce Hybrid Distillation Policy Optimization (HDPO), which augments standard RL with privileged self-distillation targeting cliff prompts. On each training step, HDPO identifies prompts where all rollouts fail, generates privileged rollouts by providing the model with ground-truth information, filters for correct solutions, and distills the teacher's token-level distribution into the student. Because teacher and student share the same weights - differing only in their input - the realizability gap is provably bounded, unlike cross-model distillation. We prove that R=1 filtered privileged generation recovers the optimal KL-regularized RL policy in the hard-threshold limit.
